- **db-access**: 資料庫存取規範（繁中）：MCP 唯讀、寫入產 SQL 交 user、LIMIT、PII mask。 載入：brainstorm 0b 偵測到 DB 關鍵詞必載。 ([fujiei22/bstack](https://github.com/fujiei22/bstack/tree/HEAD/skills/db-access) · ★2)
- **db-access**: 本番DB・ローカルDB接続の規約。DBデータを参照・更新する際に必ず確認すること。 ([OchiK/miraigikai-Shinjuku](https://github.com/OchiK/miraigikai-Shinjuku/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/db-access) · 2 copies)
- **db-access-audit**: Read-only audit of what an AI tool's database login can actually do. Covers effective identity, write and indirect grants, unmasked PII columns and attached masking, audit-trail gaps, and paths outside the database, with packs for Postgres, Snowflake, Databricks, Redshift, BigQuery, Fabric, Lake Formation, and DuckDB. Asks before connecting; never stores credentials. Use when the user asks what their agent can read or change in the warehouse, or wants an AI role's grants checked. ([kyle-chalmers/ai-data-security](https://github.com/kyle-chalmers/ai-data-security/tree/HEAD/skills/db-access-audit))
- **db-access-guard**: DB 접근 패턴의 안전성을 검사합니다. "DB 접근 괜찮아?", "레포지토리 직접 써도 돼?", "쿼리 검토해줘", "DB 설계 맞아?" 요청에 사용합니다. 코드를 수정하지 않고 위반 사항만 보고합니다. ([fsdawer/HarnessPractice](https://github.com/fsdawer/HarnessPractice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/db-access-guard))
- **db-access-review-subagent-needs-explicit-probe-budget**: Dispatching a review or verification agent with live database, gcloud, psql or bash access: give it a tool call and time budget, plus the probes NOT to re-run. Also when it gives `API Error: The socket connection was closed unexpectedly`, or has run 30 minutes. Not its sources. ([wan-huiyan/agent-traffic-control](https://github.com/wan-huiyan/agent-traffic-control/tree/HEAD/plugins/agent-traffic-control/skills/db-access-review-subagent-needs-explicit-probe-budget) · ★3)
- **db-accessor**: This skill enables the LLM to execute SQL queries against a SAP HANA database by understanding the user's intent and producing a **strictly-formatted JSON output** that describes the query to run. The JSON output is then parsed by the `db_accessor` skill to connect to the database and return the result set. ([yanggujun/open-axle](https://github.com/yanggujun/open-axle/tree/HEAD/src/skills/db-accessor))
- **db-advisor**: Automated database query monitoring and N+1 detection. Analyzes ORM query logs, detects repeated queries (N+1 pattern), suggests eager loading or batching fixes, and tracks query count per request. Use when adding DB monitoring, investigating slow endpoints, or reviewing ORM query patterns in a PR. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/db-advisor) · ★14 · 2 copies)
- **db-alphafold**: Access AlphaFold's 200M+ AI-predicted protein structures. Retrieve structures by UniProt ID, download PDB/mmCIF files, analyze confidence metrics (pLDDT, PAE), for drug discovery and structural biology. ([20minds/sennen-computational-discovery](https://github.com/20minds/sennen-computational-discovery/tree/HEAD/sennen/skills/db-alphafold) · ★1)
- **db-and-artifacts**: Query mutator.db (runs, attempts, evaluations, cells, tests, genomes, excerpts) and navigate on-disk artifacts (genomes/, evals/, mutator-work/, .briefing.md). Use when inspecting run results, debugging rounds, tracing lineage, or answering "what happened in round N".
